- **ct-map-keeper**: Cheap context-map maintainer. Use to bring a context map (`.claude/context/`, `.codex/context/`) or an `ai-context/` document up to date after code changed, from a briefing that names the map and what changed. Writes only maps and `ai-context/`. Give it a briefing from `ct brief --role=map-keeper`. ([fcoluiz/context-tools](https://github.com/fcoluiz/context-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/ct-map-keeper.md) · ★1)
- **ct-orchestrator**: Use when: coordinating one task across research, implementation, and verification agents with shared context and a single final answer. ([chaudhary-keshav/warden-multi](https://github.com/chaudhary-keshav/warden-multi/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ct-orchestrator.agent.md))

- **ct-translator**: Cheap pt-BR ↔ English translator for documentation (README, reference docs, CHANGELOG entries, map text). Use when a text already decided in one language must be mirrored in the other. Writes only the files declared in the briefing. Give it a briefing from `ct brief --role=translator --allow=&lt;target files&gt;`. ([fcoluiz/context-tools](https://github.com/fcoluiz/context-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/ct-translator.md) · ★1)
- **ct-verifier**: Cheap test runner. Use after a change to run the test command and bring back only the exit code and the literal failing lines, so a long test log never enters the main context. Call it directly with the test command; a briefing from `ct brief --role=verifier` detects the command. ([fcoluiz/context-tools](https://github.com/fcoluiz/context-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/ct-verifier.md) · ★1)

- **cta-matcher**: Adds the right call to action to every caption (comment a keyword, save, follow, link, newsletter) and checks that it matches what the creator actually says in the video.
